We present a systematic evaluation of harmful content generation across five open text-to-image models using an automated pipeline that transforms legitimate news captions into unsafe prompts targeting sexually explicit content, violence/gore, harmful stereotypes, self-harm, and hate speech. We evaluate both standard models with built-in safety mechanisms and community fine-tuned variants that bypass content restrictions. A human evaluation of 1,500 generated images shows high harmful-content generation rates: 89.2% for gore-related prompts, 47.6% for sexually explicit content, 43.6% for harmful stereotypes, 46.0% for hate speech, and 34.5% for self-harm, predominantly through graphic violence. Models show substantial capability for generating violent and stereotypical content, while community fine-tuned variants are particularly vulnerable to sexually explicit prompts. Generation quality is largely preserved under harmful prompting, producing imagery of sufficient fidelity to pose risks for disinformation and abuse; FLUX.1-dev produces clearly realistic harmful images in 30.9% of cases. We further evaluate automated moderation systems and find substantial detection gaps that allow unsafe images to evade filtering. Finally, we assess synthetic image detectors and show that models trained only on benign datasets perform worse on explicit content, while more diverse training data improves detection, highlighting semantic distribution gaps in current approaches. These findings expose limitations in current generation safeguards, moderation systems, and synthetic image detection, highlighting the need for stronger defenses against misuse at scale.

</details>


### [208] [A General Pipeline for Dense Illuminant Estimation via Physically Based Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06508)
*Luca Cogo,Gianmarco Corti,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通用可复用流程，从基于物理的3D渲染场景中生成密集光源色度图，构建了74,321张图像的大规模合成数据集，用于单光源与多光源估计模型的预训练。实验表明，合成预训练可稳定提升性能，在数据稀缺场景下单光源估计最高提升28%，多光源估计最高提升57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光源估计是计算摄影中的基础问题，用于校正不同光照条件导致的色偏。尽管基于学习的方法表现优异，但其进展受限于缺乏具有准确光源真值的大规模数据集。

Method: 提出一种通用且可复用的生成流程，通过对现有3D场景集合的再利用，从物理渲染场景中导出密集的光源色度图，在受控光照条件下系统生成逐像素的光源标注，从而降低基于学习的光源估计的数据采集门槛。利用该流程生成了包含74,321张图像的大规模合成数据集，并将其用于单光源和多光源估计模型的预训练。

Result: 基于当前最先进架构的大量实验表明，合成预训练能持续提升性能，单光源估计提升最高达28%，多光源估计提升最高达57%，在数据匮乏的情形下提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成流程为光源估计方法的预训练提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Illuminant estimation is a fundamental problem in computational photography, as it enables the correction of color shifts induced by varying lighting conditions. While learning-based methods have demonstrated strong performance, their progress is hindered by the limited availability of large-scale datasets with accurate illuminant ground-truth. In this work, we propose a general and reusable pipeline to derive dense illuminant chromaticity maps from physically based 3D-rendered scenes. By repurposing an existing 3D scene collection, our approach enables the systematic generation of pixel-wise illuminant annotations under controlled lighting conditions, effectively lowering the barrier to data acquisition for learning-based illuminant estimation. Using this pipeline, we generate a large-scale synthetic set of 74,321 images, which we employ for pre-training both single- and multi-illuminant estimation models. Extensive experiments with state-of-the-art architectures show that synthetic pre-training consistently improves performance, with gains of up to 28% for single-illuminant estimation and up to 57% for multi-illuminant estimation, particularly in data-scarce regimes. These findings demonstrate that synthetic data generation pipelines offer an effective and scalable solution for the pre-training of illuminant estimation methods.

</details>


### [209] [BrainTRACE: Tracing Longitudinal, Multimodal, and Volumetric Evidence in Brain MRI Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06571)
*Qizhen Lan,Mengchen Fan,Hang Zhang,Jingwei Duan,Moule Lin,Jialin Chen,Baocheng Geng,Xiaoqian Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出BrainTRACE，一个以报告为依据的脑部MRI纵向解读基准，用于评估视觉语言模型能否追踪临床推理所需的证据链，涵盖7,273个VQA实例与约29k个3D MRI序列体数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑部MRI解读是一个纵向临床推理过程，需要放射科医生比较系列检查、整合多序列信息、将发现定位于体积解剖结构，并转化为有报告依据的评估。现有医学VQA和3D影像基准多以孤立图像、静态体积或无依据的报告式答案来评测脑部MRI，掩盖了支撑临床有效性的证据链中的失败。

Method: 构建BrainTRACE基准，包含来自1,778名纵向患者的7,273个评分VQA实例、7,299次MRI检查和约29k个共配准3D MRI序列体积。基准按五个临床推理层级组织（从采集识别到病例级综合），并按证据需求分类（纵向比较、报告依据引用、多序列整合、体积空间证据）。支持渲染输入、3D证据条件以及分解式病例推理轨道（审计纵向证据链的六个步骤）。

Result: 对20种VLM配置的评估显示，当前系统能够识别孤立的视觉线索，但很少能将其组合成有依据的纵向解读。

Conclusion: 公开基准规范、评测列表、评分实现、评分标准和审计记录格式，以支持脑部MRI VLM评测的可复现进展。

Abstract: Brain MRI interpretation is a longitudinal clinical reasoning problem: radiologists compare serial studies, integrate information across MRI sequences, localize findings within volumetric anatomy, and translate this evidence into report-grounded assessments. Existing medical VQA and 3D imaging benchmarks capture important parts of this workflow, but often evaluate brain MRI through isolated images, static volumes, or ungrounded report-style answers, thereby obscuring failures in the evidence chain that support clinical validity. We introduce BrainTRACE, a report-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether vision-language models can trace the evidence structure required for longitudinal brain MRI interpretation. BrainTRACE contains 7,273 scored VQA instances derived from 1,778 longitudinal patients, 7,299 MRI studies, and approximately 29k co-registered 3D MRI sequence volumes. The benchmark is organized by five levels of clinical reasoning, from acquisition recognition to case-level synthesis, and by evidence demands covering longitudinal comparison, report-grounded references, multi-sequence integration, and volumetric spatial evidence. BrainTRACE supports rendered inputs compatible with standard VLM interfaces, a 3D-evidence condition, and a decomposed case-reasoning track that audits six steps in a longitudinal evidence chain. Evaluation of 20 VLM configurations shows that current systems can identify isolated visual cues but rarely compose them into grounded longitudinal interpretations. We release the benchmark specification, evaluation lists, scoring implementation, scoring rubrics, and audit-record format to support reproducible progress in brain MRI VLM evaluation.

</details>


### [210] [FrontVeg V2: A Training-Free Software Framework for Foreground-Aware Zero-Shot Plant Trait Segmentation in High-Resolution Images of Trellised Crops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06575)
*Abdoul Djalil Ousseini Hamza,Herearii Metuarea,Corentin Lothod{é},Morgane Roth,Jacem Ben Hamden,Eric Duch{ê}ne,Lionel Ley,David Alletru,David Rousseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrontVeg V2 是一个开源、免训练的前景感知零样本分割框架，用于高分辨率棚架作物图像中植物性状的分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率棚架作物图像中，邻近植被行会导致大量误检，影响植物器官和病害症状的准确分割；同时现有方法往往需要针对特定任务重新训练模型。

Method: 该流水线结合单目深度估计、基于谷值感知深度阈值的自动前景提取、分块零样本分割、基于图的分割掩码组装以及几何感知融合。当前实现集成了 Depth Anything V2 (DAV2) 和 SAM3，支持命令行批处理和 Napari 图形界面。

Result: 能够分割植物器官和病害症状，同时减少由邻近植被行引起的检测误报。

Conclusion: FrontVeg V2 为多作物、多性状数字表型分析提供了一个可复用的框架，无需针对特定任务进行模型再训练。

Abstract: FrontVeg V2 is an open-source, training-free software framework for foregroundaware zero-shot segmentation of plant traits in high-resolution images of trellised crops. The pipeline combines monocular depth estimation, automatic foreground extraction using Valley-Aware Depth Thresholding, tiled zero-shot segmentation, Graph-Based Mask Assembly, and geometry-aware fusion. This design enables plant organs and disease symptoms to be segmented while reducing detections arising from neighboring vegetation rows. The current implementation integrates Depth Anything V2 (DAV2) and SAM3 and can be used through both command-line batch processing and a Napari graphical interface. FrontVeg V2 provides a reusable framework for multi-crop, multi-trait digital phenotyping without task-specific model retraining.

</details>


### [211] [Keepsake: Selective Spatial Memory for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06588)
*Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Kunyang Li,Yuzhang Shang,Mubarak Shah,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Keepsake：一种在线、免训练的空间记忆控制器，通过“替代性”关系评估观测价值，在固定预算下动态保留不可替代帧、淘汰易替代帧，提升长时视频生成一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时相机可控视频生成依赖持久记忆维持场景一致性。现有方法分两类：全历史方法保留所有观测，导致存储与检索成本无上限；选择性构建方法虽减冗余，但保留决策一次性做出、不再重审，且忽视观测价值随记忆库演化的变化。二者共同的未解问题是：随着生成历史演进，哪些已存观测应继续留在记忆中？

Method: 核心洞见是观测价值并非固定，而是关系性的——取决于记忆库中当前可用的替代视图。提出 Keepsake：在线、免训练的固定容量空间记忆控制器。每次更新时，对保留帧与新生成帧构建位姿-外观图，结合相机位姿邻近性与视觉相似度；再用保留优先级同时刻画“强替代数量”与“最近替代的相似度”，从而持续重估记忆价值，保留替代支持少的观测，淘汰高度可替代者。仅修改持久记忆更新环节，宿主生成器、去噪调度与检索规则保持不变。

Result: 在 MemCam 与 WorldMem 上，固定记忆预算下 Keepsake 改善了 FVD 与 LPIPS。在 180 秒的 MemCam 轨迹上，仅保留 5,397 帧中的 32 帧，同时 FVD 降低 35.1%。

Conclusion: 以关系性、动态重估的视角管理固定容量空间记忆，可在极低存储成本下显著提升长时相机可控视频生成的一致性，且方法免训练、即插即用。

Abstract: Long-horizon camera-controlled video generation relies on persistent memory to maintain scene consistency. Existing systems follow two strategies to achieve this consistency. Full-history approaches retain all generated observations, causing unbounded storage and retrieval costs. Selective-construction approaches reduce redundancy, but make one-time retention decisions that are never revisited, even as an observation's value changes with the evolving memory bank. Both strategies leave a shared question unresolved: as the generated history evolves, which stored observations should still remain in memory? Our key insight is that the value of a stored observation is not fixed, but relational: it depends on the alternatives currently available in the memory bank. A view supported by many geometrically and visually similar substitutes can be relinquished with little loss of coverage, whereas an observation with few viable alternatives should remain regardless of age. We introduce Keepsake, an online, training-free controller for fixed-capacity spatial memory. At each update, Keepsake constructs a pose-appearance graph over retained and newly generated observations, combining camera-pose proximity with visual similarity. A retention priority jointly captures the number of strong substitutes and the similarity of the closest alternative, allowing Keepsake to continually reassess memory value, preserve observations with little alternative support, and evict highly replaceable ones under a fixed budget. The controller modifies only the persistent-memory update; the host generator, denoising schedule, and retrieval rule remain unchanged. Across MemCam and WorldMem, Keepsake improves FVD and LPIPS under a fixed memory budget. On 180-second MemCam trajectories, it retains only 32 of 5,397 frames while reducing FVD by 35.1%.

</details>


### [212] [VGGT-Bridge: Beyond Sequential Pose Graphs via Coarse-Stride Skip Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06594)
*Sungjae Choi,Hanna Bae,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-Bridge通过引入长距离跳跃边连接非相邻块，将VGGT的首帧尺度偏差转化为漂移校正，无需重训练即可显著降低长序列重建的漂移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT等前馈几何Transformer的二次注意力复杂度使其难以扩展到数千帧的长序列。现有的分块对齐框架仅连接顺序相邻的块，导致逐帧小误差沿链条累积形成大规模漂移。

Method: 提出VGGT-Bridge，添加长距离跳跃边直接约束非相邻块。通过在稀疏采样的粗块上运行VGGT，使每个粗块将远处的细块桥接为单一直接约束；将VGGT的首帧尺度偏差通过反向输入选定的粗块转化为漂移校正，并设计环路感知策略保持反转与已有回环闭合兼容。

Result: 在KITTI Odometry上ATE降低28.3%，Virtual KITTI上降低18.8%，Waymo Open上降低10.0%，优于SwiftVGGT基线，在所有分块对齐方法中达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 通过长距离跳跃边和尺度偏差校正，VGGT-Bridge在无需重训练的情况下有效缓解了分块对齐框架的累积漂移问题，成为当前最优的分块对齐方法。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry transformers such as VGGT reconstruct dense 3D structure from images in a single forward pass, simplifying multi-view 3D reconstruction. However, their quadratic attention complexity makes them difficult to scale to long sequences with thousands of frames. Chunk-and-align frameworks address this by splitting a long sequence into overlapping chunks and stitching their local reconstructions into a pose graph. Yet existing methods connect only sequentially adjacent chunks, so small per-frame errors accumulate along the chain into large-scale drift. To move beyond sequential edges, we propose VGGT-Bridge, which adds long-range skip edges that directly constrain non-adjacent chunks without retraining. By running VGGT on sparsely sampled coarse chunks, each coarse chunk bridges distant fine chunks into a single direct constraint. We further turn VGGT's first-frame scale bias into a drift correction by feeding selected coarse chunks in reverse, and a loop-aware policy keeps this reversal compatible with existing loop closures. VGGT-Bridge reduces ATE by 28.3% on KITTI Odometry, 18.8% on Virtual KITTI, and 10.0% on Waymo Open over the SwiftVGGT baseline, achieving the best performance among all chunk-and-align methods.

</details>


### [213] [Analysis of SWIR Imaging Detection Performance Under Adverse Environmental Conditions for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06596)
*Rohan Mehra,Alexandre Riffard,Yannis Loumouamou,Mathieu Labussière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文对RASMD数据集中配对的RGB与短波红外（SWIR）图像进行系统目标检测对比，发现总体上RGB性能相当或更优，但SWIR在四类安全关键场景中具有明显优势，应作为互补模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短波红外（SWIR）成像被视为自动驾驶的潜力模态，但其相对RGB的实际收益在多样条件下尚缺乏充分表征，因此需要系统性的对比研究。

Method: 构建配对的RGB与SWIR目标检测对比实验，覆盖RASMD数据集的四种天气条件和两种实时检测架构，并进行多种微调设置，以统一真值评估；同时提出传感器优势挖掘框架，结合多模型一致性与针对性人工检查，在少标注配对数据上识别某一模态更可靠的场景。

Result: 总体上RGB在多数场景中表现相当或更优，RF-DETR在不同条件下鲁棒性更强；SWIR在挡风玻璃眩光、挡风玻璃水滴、低对比度目标可见性以及远距离车辆检测四类安全关键情境中具有明显优势。

Conclusion: SWIR应被视为一种互补模态，用于增强罕见但具挑战性条件下的感知能力；数据集可申请获取，代码与训练权重已公开。

Abstract: Short-wave infrared (SWIR) imaging has emerged as a promising modality for autonomous driving, yet its practical benefits over RGB remain poorly characterized across diverse conditions. This paper presents a systematic comparative study of paired RGB and SWIR object detection on the RASMD dataset, covering four weather conditions and two real-time detection architectures, with various fine-tunings evaluated against a unified ground truth. Overall, RGB demonstrates comparable or superior performance in most scenarios, while RF-DETR exhibits greater robustness across varying conditions. Beyond aggregate metrics, we propose a sensor-dominance mining framework that combines multi-model agreement with targeted manual inspection to identify scenarios where one sensing modality provides more reliable detections using largely unannotated paired data. This analysis reveals that SWIR offers clear advantages in four safety-critical situations, including windshield glare, water droplets on the windshield, low-contrast object visibility, and long-range vehicle detection. The findings suggest that SWIR should be viewed as a complementary modality that enhances perception in rare but challenging conditions. The datasets will be available upon request, and all code and trained model weights are publicly released at https://github.com/comsee-research/swir-adverse-env-analysis.

</details>


### [214] [Multitask Conditional Generative Adversarial Network Enables Automatic Whole Knee Cartilage and Menisci Segmentation and Reliable T1\r{ho} and T2 Quantification Without High-Resolution Morphological Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06602)
*Ahmed Tahseen Minhaz,Richard Lartey,Zhiyuan Zhang,Jeehun Kim,Kunio Nakamura,Mingrui Yang,Jiasen Zhang,Weihong Guo,Naveen Subhas,Carl S. Winalski,Xiaojuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多任务条件生成对抗网络（MT-cGAN），可直接从qMRI回波图像合成类DESS图像并分割软骨与半月板，省去额外的高分辨率DESS扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期骨关节炎的定量MRI检测需要精确的软骨和半月板分割，但传统方法依赖耗时长、成本高的3D高分辨率DESS扫描，限制了qMRI的临床推广。

Method: 构建MT-cGAN，从MAPSS回波图像中同时合成类DESS图像并分割软骨与半月板。回顾性纳入三个队列共508个膝关节MRI体数据（361名受试者，平均年龄40.4±12.2岁，179名女性）。以DESS图像经预训练模型加人工校正生成的掩膜作为金标准，并由MAPSS回波图像计算T1ρ与T2图。采用Dice评分评价分割精度，变异系数（CV）评价T1ρ和T2定量的一致性。

Result: MT-cGAN分割性能最佳，所有软骨和半月板分区平均Dice为0.84（范围0.80–0.86），显著优于带迁移学习的最新条件GAN模型（平均Dice 0.82，p<0.001，Wilcoxon符号秩检验）。在弛豫定量方面与参考DESS方案一致性最高，CV最低（T1ρ：1.84%，T2：1.81%）。

Conclusion: MT-cGAN可准确分割软骨和半月板，并直接从回波图像提供可靠的T1ρ和T2定量。该流程无需单独的形态学DESS扫描，缩短了扫描时间，有助于推动qMRI的临床转化。

Abstract: Early osteoarthritis detection through quantitative MRI (qMRI) requires accurate cartilage and meniscus segmentation, traditionally necessitating time-consuming, costly 3D high-resolution Double Echo Steady-State (DESS) MRI scans. This study developed a multi-task conditional generative adversarial network (MT-cGAN) to simultaneously synthesize DESS-like images and segment tissues directly from qMRI echo images. This retrospective study evaluated 508 knee MRI volumes from 361 subjects (mean age: $40.4 \pm 12.2$ years; 179 female) across three cohorts. Ground truth segmentation masks were generated from DESS images using a pretrained model with manual correction, and $T_{1ρ}$ and $T_2$ maps were computed from magnetization-prepared angle-modulated partitioned $k$-space spoiled gradient echo snapshots (MAPSS) echo images. MT-cGAN was trained to jointly synthesize DESS-like images and segment cartilage and meniscus directly from echo images. Model performance was evaluated using Dice score for segmentation accuracy and coefficient of variation (CV) for $T_{1ρ}$ and $T_2$ quantification. MT-cGAN achieved the highest segmentation performance, mean Dice score 0.84 (range: 0.80--0.86) across all cartilage and meniscus compartments and significantly outperformed the state-of-the-art conditional GAN model with transfer learning (mean Dice, 0.82; $p < 0.001$, Wilcoxon signed-rank test). For relaxometry quantification, MT-cGAN demonstrated the highest consistency with the reference DESS protocol, yielding the lowest CV ($T_{1ρ}$: 1.84%, $T_2$: 1.81%). The proposed MT-cGAN accurately segmented cartilage and menisci while providing reliable $T_{1ρ}$ and $T_2$ quantification directly from echo images. By eliminating the need for separate morphological DESS scans, this workflow reduces required scan times to facilitate the clinical translation of qMRI.

</details>


### [215] [Lens3D: Target-Conditioned Visual Foveation for Fine-Grained 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06611)
*Junming Huang,Zini Chen,Shuaiying Hou,Chi Wang,Qiang Dai,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lens3D通过外部视觉辅助与知识蒸馏，提升3D大语言模型对细粒度属性与不显著物体的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D大语言模型即使面对包含相关证据的场景视频，也常忽略细粒度属性和视觉上不显著的对象与部件，细粒度理解能力不足。

Method: 提出Lens3D，包含LensUnd与LensDistill两个组件。LensUnd利用3D定位为外部2D视觉语言模型选择信息丰富且互补的视角，支持细粒度对象描述、小物体定位和细粒度对象问答；LensDistill通过详细描述监督，将细粒度知识迁移到3D LLM，使其无需调用外部VLM即可基于原生输入生成描述。同时构建LensBench评测集，含2,068个物体，每个物体配三条银标准参考描述。

Result: 在Video-3D LLM与3DRS上的实验表明，LensDistill显著提升细粒度对象描述能力，同时保持原有定位与场景级问答性能。

Conclusion: 结果验证了将外部获取的细粒度知识迁移到原生3D LLM的可行性。

Abstract: Existing 3D large language models often overlook fine-grained attributes and less visually salient objects and parts, even when relevant evidence is present in scene videos. We introduce Lens3D to improve fine-grained object understanding through external visual assistance and knowledge transfer. Its LensUnd pipeline adopts 3D localization to select informative, complementary views for an external 2D vision-language model, supporting fine-grained object captioning, small-object grounding, and fine-grained object question answering. LensDistill transfers the resulting fine-grained knowledge to 3D LLMs through detailed caption supervision, enabling captioning from native inputs without external VLM calls. We also construct LensBench, a held-out evaluation set of 2,068 objects with three silver-standard reference descriptions per object. Experiments with Video-3D LLM and 3DRS demonstrate that LensDistill substantially improves fine-grained object captioning while preserving existing grounding and scene-level QA performance. These results establish the feasibility of transferring externally acquired fine-grained knowledge into native 3D LLMs.

</details>


### [216] [Video Encoders Built on Image Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06616)
*Jusheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang,Longqi Cai,Liangzhe Yuan,Yuxiao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种紧凑视频编码路径：先用冻结图像编码器生成逐帧表示，再以问题感知选择器在帧间联合分配固定token预算，最后用轻量refiner在保留锚点上写入时序残差。在13个基准和3个VLM骨架上，该方法以约28%–35%的视觉token达到或超过全图像聚合性能，并在Qwen3-VL-32B上取得2.16倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生的视频编码路径会在视觉编码阶段就让相邻帧交互（早期时序混合），而图像路径虽保留独立帧表示，却因前传全部图像token而带来巨大的视觉token开销。作者希望探究：能否在图像表示基础上构建紧凑的视频编码器，从而兼顾帧独立性、token效率与时序建模。

Method: 将常被耦合的三个操作拆开：1）逐帧表示——冻结图像编码器产生帧级候选；2）跨帧token分配——问题感知选择器依据相关性、多样性和跨帧对应关系，在帧间分配固定token预算；3）时序交互——轻量学习refiner读取邻帧上下文，仅对保留下来的锚点写入残差更新。该方法保持源位置不变，并将视觉输出稳定在固定预算内。

Result: 在13个基准和3个视觉语言骨架上，该方法仅用约28%–35%的视觉token即可匹配全图像聚合性能。在Qwen3-VL-8B上，以1,535个视觉token达到13基准宏平均62.75，优于全Image路径的62.58（4,424 token）和原生Conv3D的59.49（2,212 token）。在Qwen3-VL-32B上，宏平均达66.28（Image为66.09），并带来2.16倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 紧凑视频编码无需早期时序混合：可以先保留帧特有证据，再进行联合token分配，最后在选择之后完成时序上下文化。这种解耦设计在显著降低视觉token成本的同时保持甚至提升性能。

Abstract: The design of a video encoder determines when frames begin to interact and which frame-specific visual evidence remains accessible to the language model. Native video pathways couple neighboring frames during visual encoding, whereas image pathways preserve independently computed frame representations but incur a much larger visual-token cost when all image tokens are forwarded. We ask a basic question: whether a compact video encoder can instead be built on image representations. To answer this question, we separate three operations that are often coupled: per-frame representation, cross-frame token allocation, and temporal interaction. A frozen image encoder first produces frame-specific candidates. A question-aware selector then allocates a fixed token budget across frames using relevance, diversity, and cross-frame correspondence, after which a lightweight learned refiner reads neighboring-frame context and writes residual updates only to the retained anchors. This preserves source positions and keeps the visual output at the fixed budget. Across 13 benchmarks and three vision-language backbones, the resulting pathway matches full-image aggregate performance while using only about 28%-35% of its visual tokens. Specifically, on Qwen3-VL-8B, it achieves a 13-benchmark macro-average of 62.75 with 1,535 visual tokens, compared with 62.58 for the full Image pathway at 4,424 tokens and 59.49 for native Conv3D at 2,212 tokens. On Qwen3-VL-32B, it reaches a 13-benchmark macro-average of 66.28, compared with 66.09 for Image, while providing a 2.16x end-to-end speedup. These results show that compact video encoding does not require early temporal mixing: frame-specific evidence can be preserved first, allocated jointly, and temporally contextualized after selection.

</details>


### [217] [RealtimeWAM: One-Step Asynchronous World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06617)
*Chengtao Lv,Jinyang Du,Shuyi Feng,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Shunzi Yang,Ruihao Gong,Shen Ren,Tianwei Zhang,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealtimeWAM 是一种极高效的世界动作模型变体，通过单步动作生成与异步推理，解决了动作去噪迭代与专家间等待两大瓶颈，实现近乎无损性能与约25倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效 World Action Models (WAMs) 采用 Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) 架构并只计算一次视频表征供动作专家复用，但动作专家的多步去噪迭代（intra-expert iteration）与视频/动作专家的顺序执行等待（inter-expert waiting）仍严重限制推理效率。

Method: 提出 RealtimeWAM，包含两大技术：1) Teacher-Anchored Consistency Distillation (TACD)，针对局部-全局误差差距，在局部一致性监督之外引入冻结教师模型多步推演端点的显式监督，实现准确的一步动作生成；2) Cross-Expert Wavefront Pipelining (CEWP)，通过分块共享视频 KV cache 让两个专家重叠执行，仅在对应动作注意力消费前进行同步，从而消除专家级等待。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 等多个基准及 Fast-WAM、Faster-WAM 等模型变体上，RealtimeWAM 均表现优越，性能近乎无损（下降<1%），端到端显著加速（如 H100 上约25倍）。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM 有效缓解了 WAM 推理的效率瓶颈，在大幅提升推理速度的同时保持近乎无损的性能，代码与检查点已开源。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) incorporate visual representations from video generation backbones to guide action prediction. Recent efficient WAMs adopt Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architectures and compute video representations once for reuse by the action expert. However, intra-expert iteration (\ie, multi-step action denoising) and inter-expert waiting (\ie, sequential execution of the video and action experts) still limit inference efficiency. To this end, we present RealtimeWAM, an extremely efficient WAM variant with one-step action generation and asynchronous inference, addressing these two bottlenecks. To reduce intra-expert iteration, we propose Teacher-Anchored Consistency Distillation (TACD) to address a local-global error gap: low local consistency error alone does not guarantee accurate final actions. TACD supplements local consistency with explicit supervision from the frozen teacher's multi-step rollout endpoint, enabling accurate one-step action generation. Additionally, we propose Cross-Expert Wavefront Pipelining (CEWP) to eliminate unnecessary expert-level waiting. It overlaps the two experts through block-wise sharing of the video KV cache, synchronizing only immediately before the corresponding action attention consumes it. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks (\eg, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin) and model variants (\eg, Fast-WAM and Faster-WAM) demonstrate the superiority of RealtimeWAM. Notably, RealtimeWAM maintains near-lossless performance (\ie, $<1\%$ drop) across these benchmarks while delivering significant end-to-end speedup (\eg, $\sim25\times$ on H100). Our code and checkpoints are available via this \href{https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/examples/realtimewam}{link}.

</details>


### [218] [Talk Like You: Imitating How You Speak in Real-Time Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06658)
*Baiqin Wang,Zhixing Ding,Jijie Li,Jiankuo Zhao,Zhen Lei,Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TalkLikeYou 框架，在语音驱动数字人生成中模仿目标人物的说话习惯，通过运动空间建模与 Flow Matching 实现实时生成，并引入新指标 PLAD 评估模仿准确性与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话头生成方法虽在视觉保真度和唇形同步上表现优异，但普遍忽视用户个性化定制，尤其是体现个人说话习惯的运动模式。这类习惯高度细微且个体间相似，难以建模与捕捉，导致生成的面部运动过于统一，缺乏因人而异的发音模式。

Method: 在运动空间中对说话习惯建模；采用仅需单步采样的 Flow Matching 实现实时性能；设计两阶段模仿学习策略以捕捉习惯间的细微差异，用户可通过数据集预设风格或参考视频指定目标习惯；并引入新指标 PLAD，将嘴部运动投影到代表性发音轴上以评估模仿精度与生成多样性。

Result: 大量实验表明，TalkLikeYou 能够实时生成高质量说话头，并在说话习惯模仿方面显著优于现有方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作将个性化说话习惯模仿引入说话头生成，兼顾实时性与生成质量，并通过新评估指标推动了该方向的可量化研究。

Abstract: In daily life, each person exhibits unique speaking habits, leading to subtle yet consistent lip-shape variations even when pronouncing the same word. Although recent talking head generation methods have achieved impressive visual fidelity and lip synchronization, they largely overlook user-specific customization, especially the motion patterns that characterize individual speaking habits. These habits are difficult to model and capture, as their motion patterns are highly fine-grained and often similar across individuals. As a result, many approaches produce overly uniform facial motions and fail to capture diverse, person-specific articulation patterns. To address this, we propose TalkLikeYou, an efficient framework that imitates how a target person speaks in talking head generation. Our method models habit in motion-space and achieves real-time performance through Flow Matching with only one sampling step during inference. We further adopt a two-stage imitation learning strategy to capture subtle distinctions between habits, allowing users to specify a target habit through either a preset style from the dataset or a reference video. In addition, we introduce a new metric PLAD that projects mouth motions onto representative articulation axes to evaluate imitation accuracy and generation diversity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TalkLikeYou generates high-quality talking heads in real-time and significantly improves speaking habit imitation compared with prior methods. The code is available at: https://github.com/BQ-Wang0511/TalkLikeYou

</details>


### [219] [Cross-dataset harmonization for robust endoscopic image analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06663)
*Romil Imtiaz,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对内窥镜图像在不同设备间泛化性能差的问题，提出一种基于参考图像的简单颜色协调方法，通过CIE-Lab空间统计变换缩小数据集间外观差异，在息肉检测任务上显著提升跨数据集性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在内窥镜图像分析中，当应用于与训练集不同内窥镜采集的图像时性能通常大幅下降。主要原因是不同内窥镜的图像颜色分布不同（取决于图像传感器和光源）。尽管此前研究已指出该挑战，但尚未被明确解决。

Method: 提出一种参考图像协调方法：从选定的参考数据集在CIE-Lab颜色空间中提取全局颜色统计量，对每张目标图像施加通道级统计变换，将其外观映射至参考数据集的分布。在柔性结肠镜和胶囊内镜数据集上，采用数据集级交叉验证协议评估息肉检测效果。

Result: 所提出的协调方法持续提升跨数据集性能，最高提升30.7%，优于相关基线和最先进方法。

Conclusion: 泛化差距的很大一部分源于低层外观差异，且这种差异无需重新训练即可缓解，说明简单的颜色协调即可有效提升跨设备泛化能力。

Abstract: A significant problem in endoscopic image analysis is that the machine learning (ML) models used for this purpose usually underperform when applied on images acquired from endoscopes that are different from those used to acquire the images of their training set. The main difference of the images originating from different endoscopes is their color distributions, which depend both on the image sensors and the light sources used. Although previous studies have highlighted this challenge, to the best of our knowledge it has not been previously explicitly tackled. This study focuses on this problem and proposes very simple but impactful method. It implements a reference-based image harmonization that reduces global appearance differences between endoscopic datasets. Specifically, it extracts global color statistics from a chosen reference dataset in the CIE-Lab color space and applies a statistical channel-wise transformation to map each target image toward the appearance of the images of the reference dataset. The method is evaluated in the context of polyp detection in both flexible colonoscopy and capsule endoscopy datasets using a dataset-level cross validation protocol. The results indicate that the proposed harmonization consistently improves cross-dataset performance up to 30.7%, outperforming relevant baseline and state-of-the-art methods. The results indicate that a substantial part of the generalization gap is driven by low-level appearance variation that can be mitigated without retraining.

</details>


### [220] [VideoTapestry: Query-Adaptive Memory Refinement for Multi-Agent Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06672)
*Yucheng Liu,Yufei Yin,Mingxiao Feng,Jiajun Deng,Wengang Zhou,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VideoTapestry——一个免训练的多智能体框架，通过由粗到细、查询驱动的记忆精炼，自适应地利用预构建的分层视频记忆来完成长视频理解，并在多个基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解对记忆需求极高，回答往往需要检索跨长时间跨度的信息。现有方法主要分为两类：查询驱动的探索（对定位误差敏感）和查询无关的记忆构建（可能遗漏问题相关的细节）。因此需要一种兼顾全局上下文与细粒度证据的方法。

Method: VideoTapestry 是一个免训练的多智能体框架。它预构建三层分层视频记忆：全局叙事上下文、事件级时序结构、细粒度关系证据。每一层配备专门的智能体，将检索与精炼限制在特定尺度的上下文中。在查询引导下，智能体重访相关视频区域，逐层用针对性的多模态观测富化记忆，并按照原有层级组装成复合的、查询自适应的记忆，在紧凑保留全局上下文的同时，沿查询相关分支保留细粒度证据以供最终推理。

Result: 相比直接使用 GPT-5.5 推理，VideoTapestry 在 LVBench、LongVideoBench (Long)、Video-MME (Long) 和 EgoSchema 上分别取得 17.2%、14.9%、9.8%、7.0% 的绝对准确率提升，在所有对比方法中达到最优（SOTA）结果。

Conclusion: 通过将预构建的分层记忆与查询驱动的由粗到细精炼相结合，VideoTapestry 有效融合了全局上下文与细粒度证据，免训练即在多个长视频理解基准上实现领先性能。

Abstract: Long-video understanding places substantial demands on memory, as answering questions often requires retrieving information distributed across extended temporal spans. Existing approaches broadly follow two paradigms: query-driven exploration, which is sensitive to localization errors, and query-independent memory construction, which may omit question-specific details. We introduce VideoTapestry, a training-free multi-agent framework that adapts a preconstructed hierarchical video memory through coarse-to-fine, query-driven refinement. The preconstructed memory organizes video content into three levels, capturing global narrative context, event-level temporal structure, and fine-grained relational evidence, respectively. To support coarse-to-fine localization and observation, we assign a specialized agent to each level, keeping retrieval and refinement within a scale-specific context. Guided by the query, these agents revisit relevant video regions and enrich layer-wise memories with targeted multimodal observations. Their refinements are assembled according to the original hierarchy into a composite query-adaptive memory, preserving global context in a compact form while retaining fine-grained evidence along query-relevant branches for final reasoning. Compared with direct GPT-5.5 inference, VideoTapestry achieves absolute accuracy gains of 17.2%, 14.9%, 9.8%, and 7.0% on LVBench, LongVideoBench (Long), Video-MME (Long), and EgoSchema, respectively, achieving the state-of-the-art results among all competitors.

</details>


### [221] [ChronoWorld: Camera-Controlled Consistent 4D World Generation via Spatiotemporal Cues and Geometric Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06687)
*Xiaoyu Zhou,Dingwei Xian,Zhenyu Wang,Yajiao Xiong,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChronoWorld框架，通过"观测—状态—反射"机制结合时空因果线索与重建先验，实现全局一致的自由视角4D场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机可控视频生成模型虽能产出视觉上吸引人的序列，但难以保持内在的4D时空一致性，需要新的方法来解决这一局限。

Method: 引入时空对极因果注意力机制（Spaciotemporal Epipolar Causal Attention），在整个生成过程中施加多视角对极约束与时间因果性；并开发重建驱动的几何反射流程，配合4D检索策略，实现动态自评估与修正。

Result: 大量实验表明，ChronoWorld在时空一致、电影级质量的4D场景生成上达到最先进性能，具有强泛化能力与跨场景高保真几何。

Conclusion: ChronoWorld通过因果注意力与重建反射机制有效提升了4D场景生成的时空一致性与几何精度，在多样场景下表现优异。

Abstract: While existing camera-controllable video generation models can produce visually compelling sequences, preserving intrinsic 4D spatiotemporal coherence remains challenging. To address this limitation, we propose ChronoWorld, an "Observation--State--Reflection" framework that leverages spatiotemporal causal cues and reconstruction priors to generate globally consistent, free-view 4D scenes. Given a context video, we introduce a Spatiotemporal Epipolar Causal Attention mechanism that enforces multi-view epipolar constraints and temporal causality throughout the generation process. In addition, we develop a reconstruction-driven geometric reflection pipeline with a 4D retrieval strategy to enable dynamic self-assessment and correction of generated outputs, improving consistency and accuracy. Extensive experiments show that ChronoWorld achieves state-of-the-art performance in spatiotemporally consistent, cinematic-quality 4D scene generation, with strong generalization and high-fidelity geometry across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [222] [GS-Pool: Object-Level Change Detection in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06688)
*Boaz Keren-Gil,James Gain,Patrick Marais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-Pool 通过将SAM2掩码提升到3D高斯并用“摄影载体”损失比较两次独立3DGS重建，实现同空间变化物体检测与分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂、博物馆等需要对同一空间不同时间重建的3DGS进行变化检测，但训练随机性使两次重建本不重合，且第二次往往照片更少，直接比较无法判断哪些物体发生变化。

Method: 方法将每次访问照片的SAM2掩码提升到渲染它们的高斯上并合并为物体池，使决策按3D物体进行；引入“摄影载体”，即用一次重建对另一次访问照片的3DGS训练损失并回传到渲染像素的高斯，结合GS-Diff几何/颜色项与蒸馏DINOv3特征，并与两次访问共有物体的证据比较以设定各场景变化阈值。

Result: 在PASLCD上达到mIoU/F1为0.751/0.846，优于GS-Diff的0.644/0.758，提升17%/12%；mIoU比O-SCD、PlenoCI、MV-3DCD分别高36%、40%、57%；在CL-Splats上mIoU为0.855，比MV-3DCD高33%。

Conclusion: GS-Pool能返回每个变化物体的高斯集合及其决策证据，供检查者在3D中复核，提升了两期独立3DGS重建下的变化物体检测与分割性能。

Abstract: Factories, museums and surveyors photograph the same space months apart and need to know which objects changed. When each visit is reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), a direct comparison of the two reconstructions does not answer this. Training is stochastic, so two reconstructions of an unchanged space never coincide, and the second visit is often a quick re-scan with far fewer photographs. We propose GS-Pool, which takes two independently reconstructed Gaussian fields of the same space and returns the changed objects in each, together with their masks. SAM2 masks of each visit's photographs are lifted onto the Gaussians that render them and merged into an object pool, so every decision is taken once per object in 3D. We introduce a photographic carrier, the 3DGS training loss of each input reconstruction against the other visit's photographs, backpropagated to the Gaussians that rendered each pixel. We combine it with GS-Diff's geometry and colour terms and our distilled DINOv3 features. This evidence is compared with that of the objects present in both visits, which sets a change threshold for each scene. On PASLCD, GS-Pool reaches mIoU/F1 scores of 0.751/0.846 against 0.644/0.758 for GS-Diff, the strongest prior method, a gain of 17%/12%. Its mIoU is also 36%, 40% and 57% above that of O-SCD, PlenoCI and MV-3DCD, and it reaches 0.855 mIoU on CL-Splats, 33% above MV-3DCD. Each changed object is returned as a set of Gaussians with the evidence behind its decision, which an inspector can review in 3D.

</details>


### [223] [Extending Dynamic World Surface Water Mapping to Sentinel-1 with AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06704)
*Rohit Mukherjee,Frederick Policelli,Beth Tellman,TC Chakraborty,Jonathan Giezendanner,Jonathan A. Sullivan,Ning Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用Dynamic World水类作为弱监督标签训练Sentinel-1 SAR模型，并融合AlphaEarth嵌入，实现全天候地表水制图，IoU显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dynamic World能提供全球10米土地利用/覆盖制图，但仅限无云观测，导致地表水制图在时间和空间上受限。需借助SAR实现全天候水体制图。

Method: 以DW水类作为弱监督信号训练Sentinel-1 SAR模型；用Google AlphaEarth Foundations年度嵌入提供空间上下文，S1后向散射提供观测时刻信息；在53个全球分布场景上用48小时内3米PlanetScope独立标注验证，并在S1S2-Water基准上测试。

Result: 仅S1模型池化水体IoU达0.77，接近OPERA DSWx-S1的0.75；加入AEF后提升至0.85。融合模型在53个场景中44个优于仅S1模型，48个超过OPERA；在独立S1S2-Water基准上达0.94，OPERA为0.87。

Conclusion: 光学土地覆盖产品可为SAR地表水制图提供可扩展的训练标签，结合嵌入空间上下文能显著提升全天候水体制图精度。

Abstract: Dynamic World (DW) maps land use and land cover globally at 10 m from Sentinel-2 (S2) imagery, but only for cloud-free observations, which limits where and when surface water can be mapped. We use the DW water class as weak supervision for a Sentinel-1 (S1) synthetic aperture radar (SAR) model so that DW-like water maps can be produced for every S1 acquisition. Google's AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) annual embedding supplies spatial context, while S1 backscatter supplies the acquisition-time observation. On 53 globally distributed scenes with independent annotations of 3 m PlanetScope imagery acquired within 48 h of the S1 overpass, the S1-only model already reaches a pooled water intersection over union (IoU) of 0.77, comparable to 0.75 for the operational OPERA DSWx-S1 product, and adding AEF raises it to 0.85. The fused model improves on the S1-only model on 44 of 53 scenes and exceeds OPERA on 48, and on the independent S1S2-Water benchmark it reaches 0.94, compared with 0.87 for OPERA. Optical land-cover products can thus provide scalable training labels for SAR surface water mapping.

</details>


### [224] [MC-Sparse: Deconstructing and Closing the Dense-Sparse Attention Gap in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06801)
*Jiarui Chen,Zeqiang Lai,Jiangshan Wang,Ziheng Ouyang,Ye Huang,Xiangyu Yue,Cewu Lu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MC-Sparse是一种免训练稀疏注意力框架，通过分析质量退化来源并提出元数据缓存与复用机制，在视频和3D生成任务中同时提升保真度与去噪速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在长序列生成（如视频、高分辨率3D资产）中延迟较高，稀疏注意力是主要加速手段，但现有方法在高稀疏度下会损害生成质量与保真度。作者希望通过受控的oracle对比，定位这种质量退化的根本来源，从而指导更优的稀疏注意力设计。

Method: 作者通过受控oracle对比，将退化归因于三个来源：token分组带来的约束、交互选择不准确、以及丢弃token时损失的注意力贡献。基于此提出MC-Sparse：免训练框架，选择单个KV token，同时将相似query组织为tile对齐的分组以适配高效GPU执行；并缓存元数据（query分组、基于精确注意力概率选出的KV索引、稠密与稀疏注意力输出的残差），在后续去噪步骤中复用。

Result: 在视频和3D生成模型上，MC-Sparse相比现有稀疏注意力基线，对稠密注意力输出具有更高保真度并获得更大的去噪加速，且无可感知的质量下降。相对稠密注意力，在Minimax-H3-Base上实现1.80倍去噪加速，在3D资产生成上实现2.32倍加速，质量损失均可忽略。

Conclusion: 通过定位稀疏注意力的三类退化来源并据此设计缓存与选择机制，MC-Sparse证明了无需训练即可在高稀疏度下兼顾生成保真度与去噪效率，为视频与3D生成中的扩散Transformer加速提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sparse attention is a primary approach to reducing the latency of diffusion transformers in long-sequence generation tasks, such as video and high-resolution 3D asset generation. However, existing methods can degrade generation quality and fidelity at high sparsity levels. Through controlled oracle comparisons, we trace this degradation to three sources: constraints imposed by token grouping, inaccurate interaction selection, and the attention contributions lost when tokens are discarded. Guided by this analysis, we propose Meta-Cached Sparse Attention (MC-Sparse), a training-free framework that selects individual key-value (KV) tokens while organizing similar queries into tile-aligned groups for efficient GPU execution. MC-Sparse caches metadata comprising query groups, KV indices selected using exact attention probabilities, and residuals between dense and sparse attention outputs, and reuses them across subsequent denoising steps. Across video and 3D generation models, MC-Sparse achieves higher fidelity to dense-attention outputs and larger denoising speedups than existing sparse-attention baselines, without visible quality degradation. Relative to dense attention, it delivers a $1.80\times$ denoising speedup on Minimax-H3-Base and a $2.32\times$ speedup on 3D asset generation, both with negligible quality loss.

</details>


### [225] [Less Context, Better Geometry: Masked Geometric Encoder for Robust 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06813)
*Zhimin Shao,Xijun Liu,Zhaoliang Zhang,Yutao Tang,Abhay Yadav,Rama Chellappa,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出掩码几何编码器（MGE）与锚点引导自适应令牌合并技术，通过训练时丢弃帧令牌并对齐全上下文教师模型，学习更鲁棒的几何表征，并在推理时加速且保持高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型依赖全对全全局注意力，带来二次复杂度、限制长序列推理，且不受约束的跨视图交互会传播被遮挡或视觉相似但几何距离远的不可靠信息，导致在遮挡与"分身视图"情形下性能下降。

Method: 提出Masked Geometric Encoder（MGE）：训练时策略性地从全局注意力中丢弃帧令牌，并从预训练的全上下文教师模型蒸馏，从而学习更丰富的单帧表征，并获得充分的中间监督。推理阶段设计Anchor-Guided Adaptive token merging，保留代表性锚点帧，同时合并其余视图的冗余令牌。

Result: 在遮挡与doppelganger视图下性能显著增强，同时在标准基准上保持高性能；相比其他高效推理方法，在有限视图设置下实现推理加速并持续保持更高的重建质量。

Conclusion: 在不完整跨视图上下文下学习鲁棒几何表征是可行且有效的，更丰富的单帧表征也为推理阶段的令牌约简提供了基础，兼顾效率与重建质量。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D foundation models has enabled rapid 3D reconstruction and camera calibration by leveraging learned 3D priors from vast amount of spatial data. However, the all-to-all global attention design leads to quadratic complexity and limits long-sequence inference; unconstrained cross-view interactions also can propagate unreliable evidence from occluded or visually similar but geometrically distant views. In this paper, We introduce a Masked Geometric Encoder (MGE), which promotes the learning of robust geometric representations under incomplete cross-view context. During training, MGE strategically drops frame tokens from global attention and distills from a pretrained full-context teacher model. This allows the model to learn an intrinsically richer per-frame representation while providing sufficient intermediate supervision to avoid performance degradation. Through extensive experiments, we show that MGE leads to much stronger performance under occlusion and doppelganger views while retaining high performance on standard benchmarks. Such a richer frame representation also leads to more effective token reduction during inference. To this end, we develop a novel Anchor-Guided Adaptive token merging technique that preserves representative anchor frames while jointly merging redundant tokens from the remaining views. Compared to other efficient inference approaches, we can achieve inference speedup while consistently maintaining higher reconstruction quality, particularly in limited-view settings.

</details>


### [226] [TAPDreamer: Transferable Adversarial Patches for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06814)
*Xuanyu Lu,Fengqing Jiang,Kaiyuan Zheng,Yichen Feng,Yaorui Ding,Yuetai Li,Zhen Xiang,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TAPDreamer，一种仅利用公开编码器、无需查询目标策略的局部补丁攻击方法，可跨任务与动作架构迁移，严重破坏世界动作模型的闭环控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型依赖摄像头输入，其视觉表征被篡改会污染跨任务与动作策略的共享表示；现有攻击需优化目标模型的输出（动作或预测未来），因此需要访问受害者模型。作者希望构造一种不依赖目标策略查询、可迁移的固定局部扰动，以揭示共享视觉编码器的安全脆弱性。

Method: 关键洞察是：补丁引起的注意力权重变化与 value 向量交互，会在补丁覆盖范围之外广播出几乎相同的表征偏移，且该偏移在不同任务观测中保持稳定。基于此，TAPDreamer 仅用公开编码器，取单个源任务的六帧图像，最大化干净与打补丁编码器表征之间的全局 L1 距离，从而构造一个固定的局部补丁（约占输入的 6.5%），无需目标策略查询。

Result: 闭环评测中，每个基准一个冻结补丁即可将 FastWAM 成功率从 97.7% 降至 0.0%（40 个 LIBERO 任务），从 90.8% 降至 0.0%（50 个 RoboTwin 任务）；匹配的随机补丁仍保留 81.5% 与 79.2% 成功率。同一补丁在两个 DreamWAM 配置上使成功率降至 2.1% 与 0.8%，在 Motus 上降至 10.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅保护下游动作生成不足以保证安全；世界动作模型的防御还必须保护共享视觉编码器，抵御持久性的局部扰动。

Abstract: World models learn to predict how their environment will evolve, making them an important foundation for general-purpose robotic control. Yet world action models depend on camera inputs whose manipulation can corrupt the visual representations used across tasks and action policies. Existing attacks on these models optimize against the victim's actions or predicted futures and therefore require access to target-model outputs. In this paper, we propose an attack, TAPDreamer, against world action models that instead uses a public encoder alone to construct a fixed local perturbation that transfers across tasks and action architectures. TAPDreamer requires no target-policy queries. Our key insight is that interactions between patch-induced changes in attention weights and value vectors broadcast a nearly identical representation shift far beyond the patch footprint, and this shift remains stable across task observations. Guided by this insight, TAPDreamer uses six frames from one source task to maximize the global L1 distance between clean and patched encoder representations. In closed-loop evaluation, one frozen patch per benchmark, covering about 6.5% of the input, reduces FastWAM's success rate from 97.7% to 0.0% across 40 LIBERO tasks and from 90.8% to 0.0% across 50 RoboTwin tasks; matched random patches retain 81.5% and 79.2% success. The same patches reduce success to 2.1% and 0.8% on two DreamWAM configurations and to 10.0% on Motus. These results show that protecting downstream action generation alone is insufficient: defenses for world action models must also secure shared visual encoders against persistent local perturbations.

</details>


### [227] [UniSlider: Perceptually Uniform Sliders for Continuous Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06831)
*David Serrano-Lozano,Duygu Ceylan,Yannick Hold-Geoffroy,Iliyan Georgiev,Javier Vazquez-Corral,Anna Frühstück*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UniSlider，将"滑块"与"强度"分离，要求感知距离随滑块值线性增长，通过少步编辑骨干上训练的轻量 LoRA 近似理想滑块，并在推理时用自适应采样做重映射，在新基准上超越既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式连续图像编辑中，滑块只是对强度参数（适配器系数、提示权重或插值因子）的简单缩放，而该强度与感知变化关系不佳：移动滑块时图像可能部分回退，长区间无可见变化，短区间又突变。重映射强度可缓解节奏不均，但前提是轨迹单调，而现有方法并不保证单调性。

Method: 作者区分滑块与强度，要求感知距离随滑块值线性增长。提出 UniSlider：在少步编辑骨干上训练轻量 LoRA，使强度逼近该理想滑块；少步采样使其可在像素空间施加该目标而无需中间真值，且骨干输出在全强度处被保留。由于低秩适配器无法使强度完全均匀，滑块被实现为推理时通过自适应采样对强度进行的重映射；训练已优化轨迹单调性，故该重映射无需额外训练或参数即可弥补剩余差距。

Result: 在包含 300 个连续编辑的新基准上，从均匀性、单调性、编辑保真度和身份保持四个维度评估，UniSlider 优于所有既有方法，并在用户研究中更受偏好。

Conclusion: 将滑块与强度解耦、以感知距离线性增长为目标，并结合少步编辑骨干上的 LoRA 训练与推理时自适应重映射，可有效实现均匀、单调且保真的连续图像编辑。

Abstract: Sliders provide an intuitive interface for continuous image editing. In current generative approaches, however, the slider is simply a rescaling of the method's strength parameter, such as an adapter coefficient, a prompt weight, or an interpolation factor. This strength relates poorly to perceptual change. The image can partially revert as the slider moves, long stretches of the range produce no visible difference, and short intervals transform the image abruptly. Remapping the strength could fix this uneven pace, but only if the trajectory is monotone, which current methods do not enforce. We therefore distinguish the slider from the strength, and require perceptual distance from the input to grow linearly with the slider value. We introduce UniSlider, a lightweight LoRA trained on a few-step editing backbone so that its strength approximates this ideal slider. Few-step sampling lets us impose this objective in pixel space without intermediate ground truth, and the backbone's output is preserved at full strength. However, a low-rank adapter cannot make the strength fully uniform. Our slider is thus an inference-time remapping of the strength, obtained by adaptive sampling. Since training optmizes to make the trajectory monotone, this remapping closes the remaining gap without extra training or parameters. On a new benchmark of 300 continuous edits evaluating uniformity, monotonicity, edit fidelity, and identity preservation, UniSlider outperforms all prior methods and is preferred in a user study.

</details>


### [228] [Anatomy-aware Fine-grained Multimodal Fusion for Laryngopharyngeal Cancer T-Staging Prediction Using CT and Radiology Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06837)
*Xingyue Zhao,Yanzhou Su,Fang Zhang,Zhanghexuan Ji,Yirui Wang,Dazhou Guo,Sibo Ju,Yuehua Cheng,Yuzhen Chen,Ming Feng,Le Lu,Tsung-Ying Ho,Jian Wang,Dakai Jin,Na Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解剖感知多模态框架，通过构建解剖结构化器官图（AOG）、器官锚定跨模态对齐（OCA）和报告增强图细化（REG），融合CT器官级上下文与放射报告，实现喉咽癌T分期的精准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的T分期对喉咽癌个性化治疗至关重要，但临床依赖有创活检，CT分期因肿瘤侵犯模式复杂而困难。现有计算机辅助方法存在两大挑战：1）结构关系建模不足，要么处理整幅CT缺乏肿瘤特异解剖约束，要么依赖费力的肿瘤分割；2）细粒度跨模态对齐困难，放射报告含器官特异侵犯细节，但全局特征融合难以将单个解剖结构与对应文本描述精确对齐。

Method: 提出解剖感知多模态框架：首先构建解剖结构化器官图（AOG），刻画原发灶与周围器官间的侵犯模式；随后进行器官锚定跨模态对齐（OCA），使每个器官节点聚合放射报告中对应的文本证据；最后通过报告增强图细化（REG）注入从报告中提取的器官特异侵犯线索，得到融合空间与文本证据的多模态器官图。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在喉咽癌T分期任务上取得了优越的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将器官级CT上下文与放射报告统一为器官图表示，本框架有效解决了结构关系建模与细粒度跨模态对齐两大难题，为喉咽癌无创T分期提供了可行且高性能的方案。

Abstract: Accurate T-staging is crucial for guiding personalized treatment strategies for laryngopharyngeal cancer. However, current clinical practice relies on invasive biopsy procedures, whereas CT-based staging remains challenging due to the complex patterns of tumor invasion. Recent computer-aided approaches face two key challenges: 1) Structural relationship modeling: existing methods underrepresent anatomically structured patterns of tumor invasion, as they either process whole CT volumes without tumor-specific anatomical constraints or rely on labor-intensive tumor segmentation. 2) Fine-grained cross-modal alignment: while radiology reports contain organ-specific invasion details, current methods that apply global feature fusion struggle to accurately align individual anatomical structures with their corresponding textual descriptions. To address these issues, we propose an anatomy-aware multimodal framework that integrates organ-level CT context and radiology reports into a unified representation for laryngopharyngeal T-staging. The framework first constructs an Anatomy-Structured Organ Graph (AOG) that captures invasion patterns between primary sites and surrounding organs, then performs Organ-Anchored Cross-Modal Alignment (OCA) so that each organ node aggregates textual evidence from the radiology report, and finally refines this graph representation by injecting organ-specific invasion cues extracted from the report via Report-Enhanced Graph-Refinement (REG), yielding a multimodal organ graph that combines spatial and textual evidence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves superior performance in T-staging of laryngopharyngeal cancer.

</details>


### [229] [Learning to Read the Contextual Tokens in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06844)
*Omer Dahary,Etai Sella,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过对MM-DiT中的上下文token进行自然语言解读的框架，并基于此发现提出Contextual Alignment训练方法以提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）在生成过程中会通过多模态注意力反复更新文本token，形成"动态上下文token"，但其功能尚不明确，缺乏对其内部表征的理解手段。

Method: 训练一个轻量级瓶颈网络，将中间层的上下文token映射到冻结大语言模型（LLM）的输入空间，从而让LLM直接从隐藏表征回答问题；并基于观察提出Contextual Alignment训练技术，显式强化上下文token中编码的视觉-语义信息。

Result: 上下文token编码了正在生成场景的丰富全局表征：生成相关的语义（包括提示中未指定的属性）在去噪早期即可读取，细节随时间逐渐可读；即使输入空提示，信息仍可解码；上下文表征可读性更高的生成往往获得更高的人类偏好分数。

Conclusion: 上下文token既是理解MM-DiT内部动态的可解释视角，也是改进生成模型的有效目标；Contextual Alignment提升了生成质量与分布覆盖度。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) jointly process visual and textual representations throughout generation. These models repeatedly update the text tokens through multimodal attention, forming dynamic contextual tokens whose function is not well understood. In this work, we introduce a framework for reading this contextual space through natural-language interrogation. We train a lightweight bottleneck network that maps intermediate contextual tokens into the input space of a frozen Large Language Model (LLM), allowing the LLM to answer questions about the emerging image directly from these hidden representations. Our reader reveals that contextual tokens encode a rich, global representation of the emerging scene: generation-specific semantics, including attributes left underspecified by the prompt, are accessible surprisingly early in denoising, while increasingly fine-grained details become readable over time. Remarkably, this information remains decodable even when the MM-DiT receives an empty prompt, showing that contextual tokens accumulate substantial image-specific information from the evolving visual representation itself. We further find that generations with more readable contextual representations tend to receive higher human-preference scores. Building on these observations, we introduce Contextual Alignment, a training technique that explicitly reinforces the visual-semantic information encoded in the contextual tokens, improving generation quality and distributional coverage. Together, our results establish contextual tokens as both an interpretable view into the internal dynamics of MM-DiTs and an effective target for improving generative models.

</details>


### [230] [S2PD: Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion for Physically and Logically Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06847)
*Jeffrey Hu,Daniel Olmeda Reino,Ayush Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion (S2PD)，在高噪声阶段用自回归扩散、低噪声阶段切换为并行扩散，兼顾序列计算能力与采样效率，从而更可靠地遵守物理与符号规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向视频扩散模型虽能并行去噪整段视频，但即便在程序化生成的海量同分布数据上训练，仍会违反物理规律和简单符号规则，说明其缺少协调相互依赖事件所需的序列计算能力。

Method: S2PD 在高噪声阶段进行自回归扩散以提供序列计算，在低噪声阶段切换为并行扩散以联合精修整段视频并减少采样时间。实现上采用两种架构：从零训练的像素空间扩散 transformer，以及对预训练视频模型通过 LoRA 微调并引入因果注意力。

Result: 在游戏、物理模拟和真实视频上，S2PD 比匹配的双向基线更可靠地遵循规则，并在时间稳定性和采样效率上优于其他串行方法。

Conclusion: 在高噪声阶段引入自回归、低噪声阶段保持并行的混合扩散策略，可有效提升视频生成的规则一致性与效率。

Abstract: Bidirectional video diffusion models denoise entire videos in parallel, yet when trained on effectively unlimited in-distribution data from procedural generators, continue to violate physical laws and simple symbolic rules. We introduce Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion (S2PD), which performs autoregressive diffusion at high noise before switching to parallel diffusion at low noise. The autoregressive phase provides the serial computation needed to coordinate interdependent events and produce valid state transitions while the parallel phase jointly refines the entire video and reduces sampling time relative to fully serial generation. We implement S2PD with two architectures: a pixel-space diffusion transformer trained from scratch and a pretrained video model adapted through LoRA fine-tuning with causal attention. Across games, physical simulations, and real video, S2PD follows rules more reliably than matched bidirectional baselines and generates videos with greater temporal stability and sampling efficiency than other serial methods.

</details>


### [231] [One Figure, Every Canvas: Editable Flowchart Relayout via Agentic Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06852)
*Shih-Chen Tseng,Chih-Hsuan Chen,Ryan Yang,Hsi-An Chen,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"宽高比自适应流程图重排版"新任务，用Parse-Style-Layout智能体流水线生成可编辑、结构保真的mxGraph XML，在100个流程图基准上内容保真度达68.6%，远超先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML论文中的流水线图需适配论文栏、16:9幻灯片、竖版海报、1:1社交图、9:16手机预览等多种画布，同一计算图在不同宽高比下若连接被悄然破坏就会误述方法。现有方法各有缺陷：图生图模型拉伸方块且拒绝极端比例，文生图智能体会幻觉内容，先解析再渲染的系统会错连边。

Method: 将问题形式化为宽高比自适应流程图重排版：给定栅格流程图与目标比例，输出结构忠实、无幻觉、可编辑的布局。提出分解为Parse、Style、Layout三阶段的智能体流水线，每阶段由主智能体配一个critic，结合确定性约束检查与VLM视觉反馈，显式校验连通性，防止连接被悄然破坏。输出为draw.io可编辑的mxGraph XML。

Result: 在100个流程图、5种宽高比的精选基准上，由Gemini 3.1 Pro评估并经人工判断验证，方法内容保真度达68.6%，先前工作仅为11.2%–41.4%。

Conclusion: 通过显式连通性校验与多阶段智能体协作，可有效解决跨画布的流程图重排版，兼顾结构保真、无幻觉与可编辑性，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Pipeline figures in ML papers must be repurposed across many canvases, including paper columns, 16:9 slides, portrait posters, 1:1 social teasers, 9:16 phone previews. Each format imposes a different aspect ratio on the same computational graph, where any silently broken connection misrepresents the method. We formulate aspect-ratio-adaptive flowchart relayout as a distinct task: given a raster flowchart and a target ratio, produce a structurally faithful, hallucination-free, editable layout. Existing methods fail characteristically: image-to-image models stretch blocks and reject extreme ratios, text-to-image agentic systems hallucinate content, and parse-then-render systems mis-route edges. We propose an agentic pipeline factored into Parse, Style, and Layout stages, each pairing a main agent with a critic that combines deterministic constraint checks with VLM visual feedback so connectivity is explicitly checked and prevented from being silently broken. Outputs are draw.io-editable mxGraph XML. On a curated benchmark of 100 flowcharts at five aspect ratios, evaluated by Gemini 3.1 Pro and validated against human judgments, our method reaches 68.6% Content Fidelity versus 11.2-41.4% for prior work. Project page: https://onefigureeverycanvas.vercel.app/

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Fine-Grained Emotion Classification from Mobile App Reviews: An Empirical Study with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03802)
*Quim Motger,Carlota Catot,Marc Oriol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用大语言模型对移动应用评论进行精细粒度多标签情感分类（基于Plutchik分类法与严重类别不平衡），比较了编码器微调、解码器少样本提示及多种不平衡缓解策略，得出解码器少样本提示基线最佳（macro-F1 0.642），而编码器结合生成式增强与损失重加权可大幅缩小差距（+0.204）且推理延迟低至三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用评论的精细粒度情感分类可支撑超越极性判断的需求工程活动（如情感驱动的议题优先级排序与面向特征的反馈分析），但该类自动情感抽取研究仍不充分，尤其面临严重的类别不平衡问题。

Method: 基于既有标注框架与取自Plutchik分类法的人工标注真值，系统比较三类方法：编码器多标签微调与二分类集成微调、开源与专有模型的解码器零样本/少样本提示，以及多种不平衡缓解策略（损失重加权、重采样、生成式数据增强），其中合成评论生成器与提示策略通过内在增强效用排序选取。

Result: 基线情况下，微调编码器（多标签0.387、二分类集成0.450）远落后于最佳解码器少样本提示（macro-F1 0.642）；但将最佳多标签编码器与生成式数据增强及正类加权损失结合后缩小了大部分差距（+0.204），且推理延迟最多低三个数量级；在稀有情感上提升最大，从完全无法检测到F1提升最多+0.501。

Conclusion: 大语言模型使面向需求工程流程的精细粒度多标签评论情感分类成为可行，macro-F1中等；最佳方案与缓解策略依赖于骨干模型与任务形式。作者公开了实验流程、合成语料与微调检查点以供复现与复用。

Abstract: Context: Fine-grained emotion classification of mobile app reviews enables requirements engineering activities that go beyond polarity-based opinion mining, including emotionally informed issue prioritisation and feature-oriented feedback analysis. However, automatic fine-grained emotion extraction from app reviews remains understudied. Objectives: Building on a previously published annotation framework and human-labelled ground truth adapted from Plutchik's taxonomy, this paper investigates how large language models can be leveraged for automatic multi-label emotion classification under severe class imbalance. Methods: We compare encoder-only fine-tuning under multi-label and binary-ensemble formulations, decoder-only zero- and few-shot prompting across open-source and proprietary models, and a catalogue of imbalance mitigation strategies (loss reweighting, resampling, generative data augmentation), with the synthetic-review generator and prompting strategy selected via an intrinsic augmentation-utility ranking. Results: Fine-tuned encoders trail the best decoder-only few-shot prompting (macro-F1 0.642) by a wide margin at baseline (multi-label: 0.387; binary ensemble: 0.450); pairing the best multi-label encoder with generative data augmentation and positive-weighted loss closes most of this gap (+0.204) at up to three orders of magnitude lower inference latency than the decoders, with the largest gains on the rarest emotions, from undetected to gains of up to +0.501 F1. Conclusion: Large language models make fine-grained, multi-label emotion classification of app reviews feasible for requirements engineering pipelines, with modest macro-F1, and the best formulation and mitigation strategy are backbone- and formulation-dependent. We release the experimental pipeline, synthetic corpora, and fine-tuned checkpoints for replication and reuse.

</details>


### [233] [Same Output, Different Gold: Measuring How Reference Choice Moves a Multilingual Benchmark Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03825)
*Parth Kulshreshtha,Shivali Dalmia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示基准测试分数中常被忽视的“参照标准”问题：由于一个未记录的聚合默认设置，官方发布的金标准实际上是部分被评系统输出的副本，导致分数随参照标准变化而波动 2.00–4.95 个 F1 点，系统间比较的配对交互项达 +2.95 点。作者提出“参照敏感性带”这一指标，主张其应与分数并列报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数的比较涉及“系统输出”与“参照标准”两端，但方法论关注几乎全部集中在系统输出上，对参照标准本身的可靠性与构成鲜有审视。作者希望测量参照标准一端对分数的影响，并检验当参照标准由不同标注来源构成时，分数与系统排名是否稳定。

Method: 利用一个六语言个人可识别信息（PII）基准的留存标注记录，其中包含两名标注者的独立标注、作为金标准发布的聚合结果，以及由独立专家对样本重新标注得到的“审阅者金标准”。在固定被评系统输出不变的前提下，替换参照标准，计算分数的变化量及 95% 置信区间，并考察两个系统比较时的配对交互效应，辅以多重检验校正。

Result: 替换参照标准后，一个输出的分数移动 4.95 个 F1 点（95% CI [3.23, 6.27]），另一个移动 2.00 点（CI [0.26, 3.37]），最差语言达 7.55 点。系统间比较也随之变化：参照标准与系统的配对交互项为 +2.95 点（CI [+1.97, +3.87]），通过多重检验校正，并直接改变了一个语言的胜负边际。根源是一个未记录的聚合默认设置：当裁决流程未触发时通常只保留一名标注者，使发布的金标准成为被评输出的部分副本。

Conclusion: 参照标准的选择会显著影响基准分数及系统间比较结果，因此参照敏感性应被视为分数的重要组成部分。作者报告了由此得到的“参照敏感性带”，给出从任意留存标注记录计算该指标的通用方法，并主张这一量度应与分数并列呈现。

Abstract: A benchmark score compares a system output against a reference, and methodological attention falls almost entirely on the first term. We measure the second. The retained annotation record of a six-language benchmark for personally identifiable information contains two independent annotator labellings, the aggregate shipped as gold, and a reviewer gold from independent expert re-annotation of a sample. Using it, we hold the scored output fixed and exchange the reference. The score moves by 4.95 F1 points for one output and 2.00 for the other (95% CIs [3.23, 6.27] and [0.26, 3.37]), and by 7.55 in the worst language. The comparison between two outputs moves as well: the paired interaction between reference and system is +2.95 points (CI [+1.97, +3.87]), survives correction for multiple testing, and changes one language's margin outright. The cause is an undocumented aggregation default that usually kept one annotator when adjudication did not fire, making the shipped reference a partial copy of an output being scored. We report the resulting reference-sensitivity band, show how to compute one from any retained annotation record, and argue that the quantity belongs beside the score.

</details>


### [234] [The Score Is Not the Structure: Brain Alignment and Cross-Lingual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03827)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 相似度分数在共享结构缺失或测量工具失效时会产生误导：语言迁移探测在部分语言上处于随机水平，脑-模型相似度经破坏目标对照后仅剩极小增量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常用相似度分数来支持"模型与大脑共享结构"或"跨语言共享结构"的论断，但很少检验当共享结构actually不存在、或测量工具本身失效时，这些分数究竟代表什么。

Method: 设置两个场景进行对照检验：(1)跨语言迁移探测，统计探测工具本身在各语言上的表现，并比较不同统计单元下的显著性；(2)训练语言模型匹配大脑响应，同时设置"对应关系被破坏的目标"作为对照，测量分数中的特异性成分与地板效应；另检验语言引导方向效应与语言距离的关系。

Result: 场景一：17种语言中有4种探测表现处于随机水平，涉及272个语言对中的64个；剔除这些语言后关系强度减半，但它们恰是最远距离的语言，设计无法分离两种效应；同一数据按272对独立计算得p=0.0006，按正确的17种语言单元计算得p=0.155。场景二：相似度从0.10升至0.34（上限0.54），但破坏对应关系后仍达0.31，仅0.028–0.068为脑特异性；无模型时破坏目标已距真实目标0.204，该地板值随目标排名/句子数变化。语言引导沿自身方向有效（17种中16种，+6.2），但与语言距离无交互效应。

Conclusion: 相似度分数在缺乏共享结构时仍可能出现较高数值。在追问"对应关系是否有用"之前，应先追问"没有它时分数还剩多少"，即须以失效工具、破坏目标和正确统计单元作为必要对照。

Abstract: Researchers often support the claim that a model shares structure with the brain, or across languages, by reporting a similarity score. We ask what such a score reads when the shared structure is absent, or when the tool that measures it does not work. We check two settings, and in both the score is not what it appears. First, a probe trained to tell grammatical from ungrammatical sentences in one language transfers worse to more distant languages, the usual evidence for shared structure. But the probe itself gets worse along the same axis: in four of seventeen languages it performs at chance, so 64 of 272 language pairs are scored with a tool that does not work. Dropping those languages halves the strength of the relationship, but they are also the most distant, and this design cannot separate the two effects. Counting matters too: the same data give p = 0.0006 when the 272 pairs are treated as independent and p = 0.155 when the seventeen languages are, which is the correct unit. Second, training a language model to match human brain responses raises its similarity score from 0.10 to 0.34, against a ceiling of 0.54. A model trained on a target whose correspondence to the brain was destroyed still scores 0.31, so only 0.028 to 0.068 of the rise is specific to the brain. With no model at all, a destroyed target already sits at 0.204 from the real one, a floor that tracks the target's rank divided by the number of sentences. Finally, steering a language along its own direction works (+6.2 over a random direction in sixteen of seventeen languages) yet shows no effect that varies with language distance. Before asking whether a correspondence helps, ask how much of the score would survive without it.

</details>


### [235] [OncoNoteBERT: A Foundation Representation Model for Natural Language Processing of Real-World Outpatient Oncology Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03829)
*Wuraola Oyewusi,Eliana Vasquez Osorio,Goran Nenadic,Gareth Price*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究基于英国门诊肿瘤语料（21,564名患者的290,026份病历），开发并评估了肿瘤学专用的BERT类编码器，发现继续预训练与定制分词器各有优势，且分词碎片化会显著影响模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的肿瘤门诊病历包含专业术语、肿瘤分期表达、治疗名称、毒性描述及机构特有的去标识化标记，通用生物医学或邻近临床语言模型难以高效表征这些内容，因此需要肿瘤学专用的编码器。

Method: 使用受治理的英国门诊肿瘤语料（肺癌与头颈癌，290,026份病历，21,564名患者），将RadBERT和PathologyBERT与两种本地策略对比：OncoNote-RadBERT（继续掩码语言模型预训练）和OncoNoteBERT（从零训练并使用肿瘤学WordPiece分词器）。评估手段包括掩码语言建模损失与困惑度、分词碎片化指标、临床术语分词、掩码token探针及探索性表征分析。

Result: 两个外部编码器零样本表现较差（RadBERT困惑度113.04，PathologyBERT为2035.03），继续预训练拟合最佳（OncoNote-RadBERT为2.10）；OncoNoteBERT困惑度为2.83，但分词效率最高，子词生育率更低、归一化序列更短。掩码token探针中OncoNoteBERT有12/13达到临床可接受预测，OncoNote-RadBERT为7/13。语料层面拟合与掩码token表现的差异部分归因于分词碎片化而非单纯学习到的语义。两种本地模型均能将机构占位符表示为单一可学习token。

Conclusion: 继续适应与定制分词提供互补收益，在将邻近领域编码器应用于肿瘤学NLP之前，表征层设计至关重要。

Abstract: Real-world outpatient oncology notes contain specialised terminology, tumour staging expressions, treatment names, toxicity descriptions, and institution-specific de-identification markers that may not be represented efficiently by general biomedical or adjacent clinical language models. We developed and evaluated oncology-specific BERT-style encoders using a governed UK outpatient oncology corpus comprising 290,026 notes from 21,564 patients treated for lung and head-and-neck cancer. We compared RadBERT and PathologyBERT with two local strategies: OncoNote-RadBERT, produced by continued masked language model pretraining, and OncoNoteBERT, trained from scratch with an oncology WordPiece tokenizer. Models were evaluated using masked language modelling loss and perplexity on the validation set, tokenizer fragmentation metrics, clinical term tokenisation, masked-token probes, and exploratory representation analysis. Both external encoders fit the oncology corpus poorly in zero-shot evaluation (perplexity 113.04 for RadBERT; 2035.03 for PathologyBERT), while continued pretraining produced the strongest fit (2.10 for OncoNote-RadBERT). OncoNoteBERT achieved perplexity 2.83 but produced the most efficient tokenisation, with lower subword fertility and shorter normalised sequence length. It also returned a clinically acceptable prediction for 12 of 13 masked-token probes, compared with 7 of 13 for OncoNote-RadBERT. This divergence between corpus-level fit and masked-token performance was partly attributable to tokenizer fragmentation rather than learned semantics alone. Both locally developed models represented the institutional placeholder as a single learnable token. These findings show that continued adaptation and bespoke tokenisation provide complementary benefits, and that representation-layer design matters before adjacent-domain encoders are applied to oncology NLP.

</details>


### [236] [SYNLAT: Syntax-Aligned Text-Latent Compression for Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03839)
*Yifeng Zhao,Hongjun Yu,Shibo Wang,Yunjiao Zhou,Zixiao Zhu,Zhipeng Ning,Kezhi Mao,Junlang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SynLat文本-隐空间CoT压缩框架，通过句法对齐单元（SAU）确定压缩边界，在12组任务聚合中匹配或超越最强基线，强压缩下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维（Long-CoT）轨迹的输出token开销很大。在预算受限时，压缩必须保留对答案至关重要的信息，因此压缩边界的位置至关重要。令牌级和固定长度边界会割裂短语、公式、局部推导等连贯片段；而步骤级边界又会把需要不同压缩动作的内容绑定在一起。

Method: SynLat将压缩边界与句法结构对齐，使用互不重叠的句法对齐单元（SAU）。由答案条件化的Teacher为单一的压缩条件化Student构建渐进的KEEP/LATENT目标；Student在推理时仅依据问题和请求的压缩级别生成混合推理内容。

Result: 在两个Qwen3 Student规模、Standard-CoT与Long-CoT两组、三种压缩级别下，SynLat在全部12个任务组聚合中匹配或超过最强评测基线，且在报告的实现压缩率（achieved-CR）选择协议下于11组中严格领先。总体增益在MEDIUM级别达3.6/2.6分，在HIGH级别达7.0/5.5分（Qwen3-8B/14B），压缩越强优势越大，Long-CoT组尤为明显。

Conclusion: 以句法对齐单元作为压缩边界、结合答案条件化教师与压缩条件化学生的文本-隐空间框架，能在受限预算下更有效地压缩长链思维，并在更强压缩条件下取得更显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) traces impose substantial output-token costs. Under constrained budgets, compression must preserve answer-critical information, making boundary placement central. Token-level and fixed-length boundaries can fragment coherent spans such as phrases, formulas, and local derivations, whereas step-level boundaries can bind content requiring different compression actions. We introduce SynLat, a text-latent CoT framework that aligns compression boundaries with syntactic structure through non-overlapping Syntax-Aligned Units (SAUs). An answer-conditioned Teacher constructs progressive KEEP/LATENT targets for a single compression-conditioned Student, which generates mixed reasoning from only the question and requested compression level at inference. Across two Qwen3 Student scales, Standard-CoT and Long-CoT groups, and three compression levels, SynLat matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in all 12 task-group aggregates and strictly leads in 11 under the reported achieved-CR selection protocol. Overall gains reach 3.6/2.6 points at MEDIUM and 7.0/5.5 points at HIGH for Qwen3-8B/14B, with larger advantages under stronger compression, particularly on Long-CoT groups.

</details>


### [237] [When Evidence Changes: Evaluating Memory Repair and Re-reading in Language-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03902)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"证据修订评估"，在ICU记录任务上比较记忆修复与重读证据的成本，发现短文本下所有记忆流程成本至少为完整重读两倍，长文本下记忆累积成本可在多次使用后低于重读，但源过滤重读始终最廉价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当支撑智能体派生事实的文档被撤销或被替换时，智能体应修复记忆还是重新读取当前证据，这一问题缺乏系统评估，作者希望通过成本对比给出答案。

Method: 在公开ICU记录的用药与问题列表任务上构建证据修订评估，覆盖撤销、替换和控制事件；比较完整重读、源过滤重读与缓存、重建、图局部修复三种记忆方案，使用两个7B模型，记忆全量提供不做检索，成本计入摄取、修订和每次使用。

Result: 短记录上局部修复的修订token比重建少5-10倍，但所有记忆流程的留出成本至少是完整重读的两倍；在约1万token记录上，记忆的平均累积成本在第2-14次使用后低于完整重读，部分源于截断抽取，而源过滤重读仍最便宜；替换研究中四项主要确证检验均未达统计显著。

Conclusion: 证据修订后智能体记忆的成本必须在完整流程范围内与源过滤重读对比评估，不能仅看局部修复的表面优势。

Abstract: When documents supporting an agent's derived facts are revoked or replaced, should it repair memory or re-read current evidence? We introduce an evidence-revision evaluation on medication- and problem-list tasks from public ICU records. Under revocation, replacement and control events, we compare full and source-filtered re-reading with caching, rebuilding and graph-local repair across two 7B models. Memory is supplied in full without retrieval, and costs include ingest, revision and every use. On short records, local repair uses 5-10$\times$ fewer revision tokens than rebuilding, yet every memory pipeline costs at least twice full re-reading in held-out conditions. In a small pre-specified development sweep, adding task-ineligible documents extended records to about 10,000 tokens; at that length, memory's mean cumulative cost fell below full re-reading's after 2-14 uses, partly through truncated extraction, while source-filtered re-reading remained cheapest. In the replacement study, none of the four primary confirmatory tests reached statistical significance. These results show why the cost of agent memory after evidence revision must be assessed against source-filtered re-reading over the full pipeline.

</details>


### [238] [General Decision Models: Benchmarking and Insights Beyond Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03935)
*Feiyu Duan,Jiayu Lin,Jia Wang,Jun Xiang,Jialiang Wu,Xinnong Zhang,Hanqi Yan,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出双语基准JEVal评估通用决策模型（如Jev）与生成式LLM在结构化判断任务上的能力差异，并揭示其在系统级组合中的可靠性瓶颈，最后提出InnerJev-4B/27B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用决策模型近期被视为替代LLM进行结构化判断的高效方案，但其可靠决策的边界、以及在系统级组合中的行为变化尚不清楚。

Method: 构建JEVal双语基准（36个数据集、11,257条实例、10个应用领域），评估25种模型配置（涵盖通用决策模型与生成式LLM），并在τ-bench、大规模社会模拟等动态系统场景测试；提出Reasoning-to-Readout自蒸馏方法训练InnerJev-4B与InnerJev-27B。

Result: 决策模型在证据充分时最具竞争力，但需专家知识或不确定性估计时较弱（常高估概率）；长程多步系统中局部决策优势被系统级可靠性失败抵消；大规模社会模拟中个体预测接近强LLM且成本更低，但用户画像更弱、聚合误差与偏差更大；InnerJev-27B在JEVal上与Jev相当，单次查询约0.1秒。

Conclusion: 通用决策模型在可证据化决策上高效且有优势，但在知识密集型、不确定性与系统级组合场景存在明显局限；通过推理自蒸馏可将LLM推理内化到单次首token决策，兼顾性能与低延迟。

Abstract: General decision models, such as Jev, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to LLMs for structured judgment and selection. But what kinds of decisions can these models reliably make, and how does their behavior change when individual decisions are composed into larger systems? To study this, we introduce JEVal, a bilingual benchmark comprising 11,257 instances from 36 datasets across 10 application domains, and evaluate 25 model configurations spanning general decision models and generative LLMs. Our results show that (1) general decision models are most competitive when decisions can be resolved from available evidence, but weaken when they require specialist knowledge or faithful uncertainty estimation: they can often identify the most likely outcome while substantially overstating its probability. (2) In more dynamic and realistic systems involving long-horizon, multi-step interactions, the advantages of fast local decision making are offset by reliability failures at the system level. on $τ$-bench, faster local decisions reduce median episode time but lower task success as decision errors accumulate over long trajectories. (3) In large-scale social simulation, decision models approach strong generative LLMs on individual response prediction at substantially lower inference cost, yet remain weaker in user profiling and exhibit larger aggregate estimation errors and systematic bias. Finally, we propose InnerJev-4B and InnerJev-27B, which internalize an open-weight LLM's own reasoning into a single-pass first-token decision through Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation, with InnerJev-27B performing on par with Jev on JEVal while answering a typical query in about 0.1 s.

</details>


### [239] [A Step Towards Forgetting: Optimiser History and the Loss of Answer Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03940)
*Valeria Ruscio,Seth Nabarro,Keiran Thompson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了优化器记忆（动量）在语言模型微调中导致遗忘的机制，发现Adam的历史梯度累积会促使旧任务答案的\"答案质量\"流失，而当前梯度则力图保护它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调时语言模型即使当前梯度试图保留旧知识，已学答案的概率仍会下降。作者希望厘清优化器的动量记忆（历史梯度）如何导致这种遗忘，将旧任务损失分解为答案间的混淆与答案集外的概率泄漏，从而揭示优化器记忆对遗忘的贡献。

Method: 在三个语言模型家族上，将旧任务损失分解为答案间混淆与概率泄漏；按梯度年龄分解Adam更新，分析历史与当前梯度的对立贡献；通过重置动量并匹配初始更新范数的干预实验，以及沿有限更新的积分分析（局部投影与曲率），考察历史的作用。

Result: 答案质量一致下降，而旧答案间的区分度通常提高；Adam更新中，累积历史倾向于促进泄漏，当前梯度则抑制泄漏；有害贡献主要来自新任务的较旧梯度，近期梯度倾向保护旧答案；历史效应变化主要由其相对于旧任务梯度的方向决定；重置动量实验表明存储历史确实影响保留，且更长Adam延续下最终旧任务损失更低，主要通过恢复答案质量实现；有限更新的积分显示大多数大幅损失增加可由局部投影捕获，而沿历史的曲率会放大某些事件。

Conclusion: 优化器的记忆可以侵蚀已学行为，即便其当前梯度正在努力保留它。这揭示了遗忘源于动量历史与当前梯度的对立，为理解微调中的灾难性遗忘提供了优化器层面的新视角。

Abstract: During fine-tuning, a language model can assign less probability to previously learned answers even when the current gradient acts to preserve that probability. With momentum, each update also carries gradients computed at earlier model states, and these stored contributions can push the model in the opposite direction. We investigate how this optimiser memory contributes to forgetting by separating old-task loss into confusion among its answers and leakage of probability outside the answer set. Across three language-model families, answer mass consistently declines while discrimination among old answers usually improves: the model becomes less likely to produce answers that it can still distinguish correctly. Decomposing Adam updates reveals opposing contributions to this loss of answer mass. Over training, accumulated history favours leakage, while the current gradient opposes it. Resolving history by age shows that the harmful contributions come mainly from older gradients of the new task, whereas recent gradients tend to protect the old answers. Changes in history's effect are dominated by its orientation relative to the old-task gradient. Interventions that reset momentum while matching the initial update norm establish that stored history affects retention, with state-dependent immediate effects and lower final old-task loss over longer Adam continuations, mainly through recovered answer mass. Finally, integration along finite updates shows that most sampled large loss increases are captured by local projections, while curvature along history amplifies some events. Together, these findings reveal how an optimiser's memory can erode learned behaviour even as its current gradient acts to preserve it.

</details>


### [240] [BAIBAICHUCHU at the NTCIR-19 FinArg-3 Task: When Is Maximum Possible Profit Predictable from Investor Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03962)
*Zong-Han Bai,Po-Yen Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BAIBAICHUCHU团队在NTCIR-19 FinArg-3社交媒体子任务中排序中文投资者帖子，开发集得分0.734但官方最好成绩仅0.517，全部12次提交介于0.4598–0.5402。事后审计发现裁判模型对看跌帖子误用长仓规则，纠正后改变87条预测中的28条但准确率不变，开发准确率反从0.680降至0.622，显示存在制度特定的语义捷径。论文将排序分解为方向性文本、波动率与期限、成对边际，发现预发帖波动率按期限缩放与MPP代理显著相关，而文本迁移几乎无预测力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FinArg-3要求按最大可能利润（MPP）排序中文投资者帖子，但团队提交结果与开发集表现存在巨大落差，且26组三轨一致样本得分仅0.500，提示需要诊断模型为何在高一致样本上失效、以及文本信号在真实市场结果中的实际预测能力。

Method: 构建三轨集成：词汇特征、FinArg-2预微调的MacBERT排序器、LLM裁判。事后审计裁判的长仓规则。将排序分解为方向性文本、波动率与期限、成对边际三个成分。使用运行极值模型预测σ√T缩放，在2026年7月独立收集的502条价格对齐帖子上检验。将预发帖波动率按帖子特定观测期限缩放（σ_pre,20√N_i），与截断期限MPP代理做Spearman相关及ticker聚类置信区间分析，并评估成对排序正确率，同时检验文本迁移的相关性与条件贡献。

Result: 官方12次提交得分0.4598–0.5402，最好0.517，开发集0.734；26组一致三轨样本0.500。纠正长仓规则改变87条官方预测中的28条但准确率不变，开发准确率从0.680降至0.622。运行极值σ√T缩放在502条7月帖子上事后观测到。σ_pre,20√N_i与MPP代理Spearman ρ=0.320（ticker聚类95% CI [0.133,0.466]），成对排序正确率61.4%。迁移文本与7月结果近乎无关（ρ=0.055），在波动率分数与立场条件下贡献甚微。开发可靠性随标注MPP差距扩大从约0.60升至0.93。早期ERAI结果0.6207排除了普遍不可预测性的简单解释。

Conclusion: 评估应联合考虑文本、市场制度、历史波动率、期限和成对组成。MPP是立场感知的，长仓规则会系统性扭曲看跌帖子的排序；同时文本信号可迁移性弱，而基于波动率与期限缩放的信号更具稳健预测力，开发集的高分可能源于制度特定的语义捷径而非真实泛化能力。

Abstract: The BAIBAICHUCHU team participated in the Social Media Subtask of NTCIR-19 FinArg-3, ranking Chinese investor posts by Maximum Possible Profit (MPP). A three-track ensemble of lexical features, a FinArg-2-pre-finetuned MacBERT ranker, and an LLM judge reaches 0.734 in post-grouped development evaluation, but our best official run scores 0.517. All twelve submitted runs lie between 0.4598 and 0.5402, and our 26 unanimous three-track pairs score 0.500.
  A post-hoc audit finds that the submitted judge applied a long-only rule to bearish posts although MPP is stance-aware. Correcting it changes 28 of 87 official predictions without changing accuracy, yet lowers development accuracy from 0.680 to 0.622: a regime-specific semantic shortcut improved validation fit.
  We decompose ranking into directional text, volatility and horizon, and pairwise margin. A running-extremum model predicts $σ\sqrt{T}$ scaling, observed ex post in 502 price-aligned posts from a separate July 2026 collection.
